# Seven boundary points: the lower point in the ABH ear

**Theorem.** In the all-interior seven-contact normalization, suppose
one free point G lies in HABC, two free points O,P lie in HCEF,
and G lies inside ABH. It is impossible that every actual triangle has unsigned
doubled area at least s=93/1000. Under that threshold hypothesis,
ONE_LOWER_POINT.md excludes simultaneous occupancy of the two lower
ears, so the present ear has ACG>0 and HBG<0. This complete lower-cell child therefore
has minimum ordinary triangle area less than 93/2000<A*.

The normalization, all scalar bounds, and every geometric and algebraic
rule used below are proved in N7_ONE_LOWER_SETUP.md. In particular
A=(0,a),B=(b,0),C=(c,0),D=(1,d),E=(1,e),F=(f,1),H=(0,h), with
0<a<h<1,0<b<c<1,0<d<e<1,a<=e<=h,b<=f<=c. The three other actual
points are G=(g,z),O=(x,y),P=(u,v). Permute O,P so HOP<0; the fixed
HEF capacity-one argument then gives EHP>0. This permutation does not
use another physical square reflection.

The proof has 55 exact cubic leaves and 4
geometric leaves, with 21,373 positive integer entries and
1,993 explicitly printed sign deductions. Every leaf of
the displayed binary trie is covered. The optional standard-library
checker reads this manuscript, reconstructs all factors, and performs
only exact rational arithmetic.

## 1. Exhaustive branch ledger

The two lower-cell signs are fixed by the theorem. Every remaining
branch is a strict sign of an actual triangle. At every internal node
the two child paths have opposite signs of the same triangle; since
all actual unsigned areas are at least s, no zero case is missing.
Rows of type P are polynomial contradictions; rows of type G are direct
geometric or scalar contradictions. The source box and affine-list
identifiers are defined in Section 2.

## 2. Exact affine factors and squares

The coordinate order is a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,z,x,y,u,v. Each source-box
line prints its thirteen lower endpoints, a vertical separator, and
its thirteen upper endpoints. Every such interval contains the proved
refined box of this lower-cell root. Thus its affine factors are valid
even when they retain weaker bounds. All polynomial upper bounds below
are evaluated on the refined root box, as specified in the setup note.

```n7-l2u-boxes
B01 119/1000 7347/69700 2697/5000 119/1000 129642/311765 7347/69700 7347/69700 301/500 119/1000 7347/69700 119/1000 7347/69700 119/1000 | 843/1000 535/907 883/1000 353/500 907/1000 883/1000 883/1000 1 907/1000 883/1000 907/1000 883/1000 907/1000
B02 17/100 42/125 173/250 17/100 471/1000 42/125 14/125 98/125 17/100 14/125 17/100 14/125 17/100 | 137/200 535/907 883/1000 303/500 907/1000 883/1000 397/1000 1 653/1000 883/1000 907/1000 883/1000 907/1000
```
The first 36 affine factors are fixed in the setup note. Each following
line lists, in order, the additional named factors appended to them.
Their conditional triangle-containment hypotheses are checked from that
row's path and preceding deductions.

```n7-l2u-facets
F01 none
F02 Gsum UHC_O_ear UHC_P_ear PHE_cap
F03 Gsum UHC_O_ear UHC_P_ear PHE_cap LOWER_ACH_ratio
F04 Gsum UHC_O_ear UHC_P_ear PHE_cap LOWER_ACH_ratio HCE2_e
F05 Gsum UHC_O_ear UHC_P_ear PHE_cap LOWER_ACH_ratio HCF2_c HCF2_f HCE2_e
```
A square line gives its name, positive denominator D, and fourteen
integer coefficients c0,...,c13. It denotes the square of
(c0+sum(c_i*coordinate_i))/D, which is nonnegative everywhere.

## 3. Complete fixed proofs

The readable deduction records use the rules in the setup note. Polygon
words give the actual cyclic order; all required positive edge and triple
signs must already be present. A cap record specifies a fixed containing
triangle, a shifted-box bound, or one actual fan with two stated source
costs. The cap is checked against the rational constant in its factor.
Each sum line is a positive integer weight followed by one factor or a
product of factors. All costs and spread constants are explicit.

Every leaf contradicts the threshold hypothesis, and the strict-sign
trie covers the entire lower-cell case in the theorem. The other three
lower-cell cases are separate children; this theorem alone does not
assert completion of the whole N7 family.
